# Full match investigations

Every round and engine transition was processed. The checkpoint tables below are comparisons for the narrative, not the extraction schedule. Full round states, ordered transitions and personal fog-limited turn views are preserved for arbitrary-round inspection. Each dossier includes an authored causal interpretation, counterexamples and exact turning-point windows. No alternative policy was simulated in this study.

Physical bodies and draft picks differ: Cassi & Issac is one pick and two bodies. Gold is lifetime earned gold, not farming income alone. Conversion divides lifetime submitted by lifetime collected; enemy gem transfers can be collected more than once across the match.

## 01. 02arena — 01018bb3-255d-49b6-a23b-ef415deaff3b

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/1c885b74-a8d2-4177-b8c5-492c0e977942) · match `01018bb3-255d-49b6-a23b-ef415deaff3b` · 2026-10-29T13:00:04.569Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1538 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Sponge(Albert and Bob) wins 1001–186.**

Sponge wins with Ryth, Vendetta and Marcella against the more fashionable Vesper–Heart–Vendetta trio. Claudus actually leads earned gold 480–328 at R100 and gets the first champion kill at R100. That advantage does not survive: by R250 Sponge leads income 1106–803 and takes the permanent score lead at R216. Marcella's Jungle completion at R29 precedes Treasure at R214, whereas Claudus's Vesper opens Treasure at R36. The decisive pattern is sustained survivable production and conversion, not first blood or an economy emblem in isolation. Twenty-four versus two physical deaths deprive Claudus of subsequent farming cycles. A counterfactual worth testing is retaining its first-income lead with a safer second objective, but no replacement action has been simulated. This is direct evidence against calling the popular trio intrinsically dominant on every three-pick map.

## 02. 00contest — 089ea886-bb77-4a84-8c00-428622008952

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/9468c3d0-317c-41a0-8b53-d1c912bb5d84) · match `089ea886-bb77-4a84-8c00-428622008952` · 2026-10-29T13:00:04.549Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 2000 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Mite wins 400–0.**

The strongest-ranked team is decisively countered here. Mite wins 400–0 at the round limit despite collecting only 400 against 600 gems. Mite's first bank does not happen until R1779; its early advantage is repeatable gold production and control, rather than early score. Prism kills Vesper at R67, and the rank-one side eventually suffers 62 physical deaths. Its Vesper loses a 200-gem bag at R1752, followed by Marcella losing 200 at R1885. Mite banks 200 at R1779 and another 200 at R1917. The late delivery sequence converts control into the actual win condition. The native score race supports a denial/late-conversion interpretation; it does not reveal whether Mite explicitly planned a round-limit strategy. No fixed early-bank rule explains this winner.

## 03. 00expanse — 18e53453-37f1-4682-95ad-edea73d52eee

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/de804efa-6c78-42b1-86c2-7d3cdeb2364b) · match `18e53453-37f1-4682-95ad-edea73d52eee` · 2026-10-29T13:00:04.559Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 941 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Intercontinental Fish wins 1039–848.**

Intercontinental Fish wins a relatively low-death resource race. Both teams finish with two physical deaths, and the eventual winner trails earned gold and equipment at R100, R250 and R500. At R500 the equipment comparison is 2880–3540 while submitted score is already 358–298 in Fish's favour. The first champion kill is only R688, after the scoring advantage is established. Fish banks all 1039 collected gems, and Varg contributes 299 submitted gems; the closing sequence banks 27, 30 and 44 on R939–941. This isolates conversion and coverage as more credible explanations than superior combat or a mandatory early-Treasure opening. The loser does have a successful economy Vesper; its R54 Treasure is much earlier than Fish's Vesper R290 Treasure. A controlled experiment would hold routes constant and change delivery scheduling, rather than copying the winner's draft alone.

## 04. 00dunes — 24184b1f-3b1f-4d40-8fef-5ce92a64f3e2

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/79d78213-2bf0-40b3-884f-d36128a59dd0) · match `24184b1f-3b1f-4d40-8fef-5ce92a64f3e2` · 2026-10-29T13:00:04.578Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 998 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**print dont int wins 1003–551.**

print dont int creates an early production lead: R100 gold 429–144 and score 43–0. Marcella reaches Treasure at R93; the opposing Marcella waits until R207, while several opposing bodies never complete an emblem. Its first kill at R76 removes the enemy Heart, but the score gap is only 284–275 by R500, so the opening does not by itself decide the match. The permanent score lead starts at R344 and the late race expands to 1003–551. The winner's seven deaths versus twelve help preserve productive time, while gold and scoring are distributed unevenly among bodies. The losing Marcella still banks 390, showing that one effective carrier cannot necessarily compensate for weak production elsewhere. The general lesson is to assess the whole route-and-delivery loop, including secondary bodies, before attributing success to the first economy purchase.

## 05. 01boss_battle — 3b30c86a-59c6-4ba9-985b-e771210c3f51

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/39296de8-ada3-426e-aeb6-b8919c3b799b) · match `3b30c86a-59c6-4ba9-985b-e771210c3f51` · 2026-10-29T13:00:04.541Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 772 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**newer team wins 1000–0.**

The critical causal chain is gate access followed by delivery. Newer team clears the 40000-HP group 16 at R533 and Vendetta banks 309 at R545. Varg later delivers 295 at R617 and 249 at R752, totalling 544; Vendetta and Willow supply the other 456. Prism banks nothing and ends with 989 gems, a mechanically meaningful source of extra Focus. Ad Astra has 1399 lifetime collected gems but zero submitted. Its Marcella loses 608 at R672. Equal final physical deaths (three each) do not explain the 1000–0 score. Newer team already leads R100 gold 865–170 and R500 equipment 7500–4410, so the gate is supported by an earlier scaling advantage. The gate clears again at R748 with Prism credited. This supports distinct scaling, gate-damage and delivery roles; it does not establish that every team should retain a 989-gem Prism or that an earlier unsupported gate rush would work.

## 06. 02tangle — 19ab1bcf-b056-450d-ae4c-09e435e3bfc7

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/e7e64ffe-5a88-4c27-99b9-22985573da8a) · match `19ab1bcf-b056-450d-ae4c-09e435e3bfc7` · 2026-10-29T12:30:04.623Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1244 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**NEETCoders wins 1145–833.**

NEETCoders postpones its first bank until R550, while policy wonk banks at R99. At R500 policy wonk leads 94–0 in score and 1935–1861 in earned gold, with close equipment. NEETCoders nevertheless produces 1198 collected gems and 7670 gold by the end against 970 and 5064, then takes the permanent score lead only at R1243. Vesper and Ryth each submit 159 at R1243–1244, producing an immediate finish. The winner loses four bodies versus one, so minimising deaths alone would choose the wrong policy. Its earlier Prospector-oriented builds are a candidate contributor to later production, but topology and assignments remain confounded. The evidence supports recognising accumulated delivery capacity and simultaneous arrival, not concluding that all banks should be delayed. A useful counterfactual tests policy wonk's late denial or earlier completion of another capture cycle.

## 07. 01islands — 81686d7d-25e8-43e2-9627-7a7501c421aa

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/eb70c3df-f864-45db-81d4-a806ca246273) · match `81686d7d-25e8-43e2-9627-7a7501c421aa` · 2026-10-29T13:00:04.597Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 840 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**policy wonk wins 1027–887.**

policy wonk wins with less lifetime earned gold (7007 versus 7398) and less equipment at the main checkpoints. It is already ahead 133–4 in submitted gems at R250 and 441–334 at R500, and never loses the score lead after its first R56 bank. Both sides have very few deaths, and the first champion kill is R696, so a combat-first explanation is weak. Policy wonk's Marcella banks 490, including the final 30 at R840; Supaswag's strongest earner is Vesper, which does not by itself create a winning score. This is a clean warning against optimising equipment value while overlooking delivery latency across fragmented terrain. Exact crystal and movement records are available, but one match cannot establish a universal island draft or prove that the winner used a particular pathfinding algorithm. Test risk-aware altar routing separately from item changes.

## 08. 01fracture — 5e95c8e8-4cf5-470d-ab7a-0948e6c620b9

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/604e7d3e-c8cb-4d6a-8cea-aa664cba01ae) · match `5e95c8e8-4cf5-470d-ab7a-0948e6c620b9` · 2026-10-29T12:40:05.720Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 979 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Ad Astra wins 1001–725.**

Ad Astra initially trails economic development: at R250 gold is 931–1310 and equipment 900–1020, and at R500 its score advantage is only 233–229. The first champion kill at R169 is against Ad Astra's Ren; the winner eventually suffers twelve physical deaths versus six. Its Vesper nonetheless grows into the main economic and scoring actor, earning 5286 and submitting 585. The durable scoring lead emerges only at R840, followed by Vesper's 85-gem bank at R947 and Ren's finishing 19 at R979. The evidence favours recoverable production and conversion over a blanket rule against costly engagements. Ad Astra's early Prospector Vesper at R50 and Capture at R245 fit a specialised production role, but do not isolate item causality. A prospective test should preserve those assignments while changing one risky fight or one delivery choice.

## 09. 00reverse_spiral — 3f460d2d-6c43-48b9-985d-cbc63206c2ad

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/1065b8d4-17cf-4519-824e-727e44ea884b) · match `3f460d2d-6c43-48b9-985d-cbc63206c2ad` · 2026-10-29T12:10:04.364Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1377 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Sponge(Albert and Bob) wins 1010–335.**

Sponge's unusual Stellar–Heart–Ryth–Marcella composition starts behind in resources and remains behind at R500: 869–1160 gold and 79–172 submitted gems. Marcella's early Jungle at R107, Treasure at R175 and first kill at R127 provide one plausible resilient carry loop, but the match stays recoverable for both teams for hundreds of rounds. Sponge takes its permanent score lead at R754 and ultimately wins 1010–335 with ten versus twenty-four physical deaths. Marcella contributes 516 banks and 4784 gold; Stellar finishes with 22 at R1377. This is a counterexample to dismissing rare champions or declaring an early economic deficit terminal. It is also not evidence that Stellar causes the win. The transfer principle is sustaining a viable carrier and secondary scoring route after an imperfect opening, while measuring the cost of exposed travel.

## 10. 00eight — 4942b190-2ee0-4db2-bf42-1a1839251bab

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/4a825ae4-7dbf-42ea-825b-fc77e629afa7) · match `4942b190-2ee0-4db2-bf42-1a1839251bab` · 2026-10-29T11:30:06.006Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1071 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**print dont int wins 1004–361.**

Supaswag has the R100 economic and first-bank lead, yet print dont int takes the lasting score lead at R152. Its Marcella opens Treasure at R49 and Durability at R121, wins the first champion kill at R115, and ultimately banks 830 of the team's 1004 score. This strongly concentrates both conversion and the bank-gold feedback on one actor; Marcella's total earned gold is 6774. At R500 team gold is still slightly lower than Supaswag's, despite a score advantage of 309–112. Supaswag's Marcella loses 59 at R538 and 67 at R713. These are concrete opportunities for carrier protection and target selection. The data supports a strong role-specific scoring engine, not a universal instruction to funnel every capture to Marcella: several other maps are won through distributed carriers, and travel/fight costs of forced funneling remain untested.

## 11. 00anvil — 5e9a9b64-ab62-49fa-971c-e5209f23958b

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/2bfe6744-c88c-4836-85d3-4d6a6a32e219) · match `5e9a9b64-ab62-49fa-971c-e5209f23958b` · 2026-10-29T12:40:05.702Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1194 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**newer team wins 1145–33.**

Newer team establishes a bank lead at R25 and never relinquishes it. At R100 gold is 513–323 and equipment 420–150; by R500 score is 429–12 and gold 3092–1360. The two teams share Vesper, Heart and Marcella, making the result especially useful as a warning that draft identity is insufficient. Newer team's early Prospector sequence and fast delivery are visible; Fish's Treasure-oriented opening does not prevent repeated deaths and scoring stagnation. Fish suffers sixteen physical deaths versus four, including a 25-gem Heart loss at R317. Vesper contributes 504 submitted gems and the final 200 at R1194. The likely mechanism is repeated productive capture-and-delivery cycles under favourable survival, rather than a single terminal combat. The opening economy, route geometry and micro are still inseparable observationally; test them in isolated variants.

## 12. 02stalactites — 3a7d1da4-9c60-493f-8ccf-144e9d6a53e7

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/005de57f-7db1-441b-ba9d-fc06434ef9c3) · match `3a7d1da4-9c60-493f-8ccf-144e9d6a53e7` · 2026-10-29T12:30:04.607Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 546 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**hello wins 1014–154.**

hello wins a high-transfer gem contest with Tavi, Vendetta, Marcella and Ryth. Tavi gets the first champion kill at R20, and hello banks 168 by R100 while Claudus has zero earned gold. Large bags then change hands: hello's Ryth loses 287 at R434, and Claudus's Tavi loses 287 at R508. hello's Marcella submits 210 at R533 and Vendetta submits 287 at R546. The 1833 lifetime gems collected should not be read as 1833 newly generated map gems; stolen bags can be counted again on receipt. This is a concrete case where kill/recovery timing and delivery dominate conventional economy scaling. The counterfactual opportunity is protecting or immediately converting the recovered 287, not maximising aggregate collection. Tavi's field shopping is a legal advantage to investigate here, but the match does not isolate its effect from early target selection.

## 13. 01broken_curve — 44203a7e-7b7c-4b17-9341-df9092d45b71

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/ff16d5b2-7494-41d0-be75-b3969b6414fd) · match `44203a7e-7b7c-4b17-9341-df9092d45b71` · 2026-10-29T12:00:04.734Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 776 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Mite wins 1003–718.**

Mite wins 1003–718 with slightly less lifetime gold (5863–5887). Its three-pick Vesper–Heart–Vendetta composition has no Marcella, while the rank-one opponent uses Vesper–Heart–Marcella. Mite's Jungle-first bodies grow into Monster/Capture complements; the opponent uses early Treasure and Prospector. Mite has a lasting bank lead after R234 and no deaths. The most consequential late transfer is Vesper's 251-gem loss at R759; the match ends at R776. That event makes bag protection a stronger explanatory variable than raw item value, although Mite was already ahead before it. A legal retreat or earlier safe bank might protect that bag, but its exact outcome is unsimulated. This case joins contest in rejecting the assumption that the snapshot's rank-one team exposes an unbeatable universal policy.

## 14. 00vault — b739a862-e41b-4cf7-ae02-834e9d6ef759

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/4aa3fb87-19c3-4097-9b8a-99c6c86b908b) · match `b739a862-e41b-4cf7-ae02-834e9d6ef759` · 2026-10-29T13:00:04.592Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 354 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**NEETCoders wins 1380–20.**

NEETCoders wins rapidly, 1380–20 at R354, despite trailing gold 120–266 at R100. It banks first at R72, then reaches 343 score by R250 while meow has 20. meow's Marcella loses a 347-gem bag at R267. NEETCoders closes with Vesper 171 at R348, Heart 172 at R351, and Vendetta 518 at R354. The final overshoot above 1000 is legal: a completed tribute submits the current bag. Neither this fast finish nor its large banks can be explained by purchasing many late emblems. Safety around a single huge bag, routing and securing a sequence of deliveries are the operative questions. The final source-side loss is not proof that banking 347 earlier was always legal or safe; altar accessibility and visible pressure must be checked before prescribing that action.

## 15. 00swirl — 3d98b425-4c45-49c5-93e3-c45df418e80d

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/6520e8ed-d851-47ed-b619-bd129561fad4) · match `3d98b425-4c45-49c5-93e3-c45df418e80d` · 2026-10-29T12:30:04.617Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1021 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Trzy Kwaterki wins 1009–447.**

Trzy Kwaterki loses the first champion kill at R30, but banks at R36 and retains the score lead thereafter. Its Vesper opens Prospector at R30 and Treasure/Jungle at R76, then becomes extraordinarily dominant: 13455 earned gold and 820 submitted gems. At R250 its team still trails earned gold 1887–2020, so the later runaway is a repeated-cycle effect rather than an already overwhelming start. Supaswag suffers twenty-six deaths versus fifteen, and its Vesper loses bags again at R56 and R882. This supports measuring who repeatedly receives valuable rewards and converts them, rather than treating total travel or the first kill as decisive. Prospector's cluster-only mechanic makes this a resource-taxonomy question as well as a build question. The late fuel-rollover count is high even for the winner; eliminating all rollovers is not demonstrated to be necessary for victory.

## 16. 00quarry — d8d3355d-2dce-46da-951f-b3fbff672f71

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/10ce7d0f-7cbd-46b0-a328-78b79c320d90) · match `d8d3355d-2dce-46da-951f-b3fbff672f71` · 2026-10-29T12:40:05.726Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1044 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**policy wonk wins 1035–447.**

policy wonk starts banking later (R225 versus R62) and trails gold/equipment at R100, R250 and R500, yet wins 1035–447. Marcella's Treasure at R110, Prospector/Jungle at R232 and eventual 606 submitted gems form the central observed conversion loop. The score lead becomes permanent at R368; the first champion kill at R191 is actually against policy wonk's Vendetta. Late enemy losses of 39 at R839 and 43 at R902 help prevent recovery. This is evidence against both first-bank timing and early equipment alone as universal success metrics. The winning hypothesis is sustainable delivery by a protected scoring actor, with enough production to keep doing it. It remains possible that local route control rather than a better banking threshold explains much of the difference; a future experiment must distinguish those mechanisms.

## 17. 00fragment — 360f42eb-f54d-44b7-b390-43e1dc446755

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/934d4798-e799-4022-a314-b8a27037aa14) · match `360f42eb-f54d-44b7-b390-43e1dc446755` · 2026-10-29T12:30:04.639Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1767 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Sponge(Albert and Bob) wins 1014–128.**

meow initially leads gold 504–330 at R100 and banks before Sponge. Sponge's three-pick Ryth–Vendetta–Marcella team reverses the economy by R250, takes a permanent score lead at R207 and finishes 1014–128. The first champion kill is Vendetta eliminating meow's Heart at R146. meow ultimately suffers thirty-eight physical deaths against eleven; this repeated downtime prevents the opening advantage from regenerating into score. Sponge's Marcella opens Jungle at R55 and Treasure at R166 and later contributes 7661 gold and 577 score. The lesson resembles arena: a comparatively unfashionable draft plus survivable repeated cycles can beat the common trio. A more defensive second objective is a plausible alternative for meow, but no exact counterfactual is established. Sparse camps increase the cost of returning repeatedly to a losing shared fight.

## 18. 00lanes — 2ccd0703-1878-4c92-b158-50ddb56098bb

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/efd11894-c56a-49c4-8ce9-5d601027cbd1) · match `2ccd0703-1878-4c92-b158-50ddb56098bb` · 2026-10-29T12:20:04.711Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 949 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**hello wins 1022–668.**

hello's winning draft includes Tavi and omits Heart, while Nyx uses the common Vesper–Heart–Vendetta–Marcella–Ryth five. Nyx leads gold 455–323 at R100, but hello reverses that lead by R250 and grows to 3512–2580 by R500. Score remains close at R500 (314–311), so the income advantage is not immediately a delivered win. hello takes the lasting score lead at R468, wins the first champion kill at R528 and finishes through distributed banks, including Vendetta's last 23 at R949. Five actors' development and survival matter more than one composition's popularity: hello has two deaths against seven. The map supports a candidate division into parallel farming routes and mobile spending, but neither a lane assignment algorithm nor the marginal value of Tavi is uniquely identified from this single match.

## 19. 00split — 05f117b9-6585-4fce-8b59-aea44b8945ca

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/731cced1-1991-49fd-9ead-7e5d9588b764) · match `05f117b9-6585-4fce-8b59-aea44b8945ca` · 2026-10-29T12:40:05.706Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 654 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**NEETCoders wins 1015–511.**

NEETCoders converts early resources into a score lead before any convincing equipment advantage appears. At R100 it has score 87–0 despite lower earned gold (458–484), then at R500 score 574–300 despite lower equipment (1950–2040). Physical deaths end equal at three each. Marcella opens Prospector at R71, Jungle at R127 and Treasure at R211, and eventually supplies 446 score and 2323 gold. The first champion kill goes against its own Vendetta at R156, so first blood is again misleading. The opposing Vesper loses 71 at R512, while the winning side keeps converting and finishes with banks on R653–654. This supports a production-plus-delivery policy with champion-specific allocation; it does not show that all champions should take the same Prospector opener. Keep the current-source recall findings separate from this elite replay.

## 20. 00monolith — ab6c4fa4-a95e-4170-a7b3-03a35e01573e

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/604e7d3e-c8cb-4d6a-8cea-aa664cba01ae) · match `ab6c4fa4-a95e-4170-a7b3-03a35e01573e` · 2026-10-29T12:40:05.713Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 956 rounds · elite. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Ad Astra wins 1029–634.**

Ad Astra's distinctive Heart–Ren–Marcella–Vesper–Ryth draft recovers from a small early economic deficit. At R250 it holds a small score lead with less equipment (1170–1590); the advantage becomes durable only at R424. Marcella gets the first champion kill at R234, and Vesper eventually submits 509 including a late 270 at R943. Supaswag's nine deaths versus two, together with losses on Vendetta and Vesper, erode its ability to convert repeated captures. Early Prospector/Capture on Marcella and Hunter/Prospector/Capture on Vesper show role-specific investment rather than a fixed universal build. The presence of monoliths is not sufficient reason to recommend rushing every one: route value, defensive damage and access still need explicit evaluation. Ren's appearance in two Ad Astra wins supports further testing, not a broadly established counter tier.

## 21. 00elkring — 346e719d-f803-4034-b2c5-28348fb857a0

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/eb70c3df-f864-45db-81d4-a806ca246273) · match `346e719d-f803-4034-b2c5-28348fb857a0` · 2026-10-29T13:00:04.594Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 921 rounds · loss. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Supaswag wins 1006–895.**

Supaswag wins 1006–895 despite collecting fewer gems (1116–1201) and suffering more physical deaths (15–11). The teams are tied at 176 score at R250, and policy wonk leads 473–418 at R500. Supaswag takes the lasting lead only at R766. Its Marcella has early Treasure at R32, Hunter/Durability at R90, and eventually 6422 gold and 797 submitted gems. In contrast, policy wonk's Vendetta loses 69 at R747 and Ryth 57 at R861. This is a particularly useful elite-loss case: apparently superior gross production and survival do not guarantee the score race. A carrier-oriented economy and late delivery protection are more plausible explanations than champion rarity. The exact policy threshold for banking cannot be inferred from final bags alone; the full bank-start and fog-limited turn context should govern a future imitation test.

## 22. adgato_gemcombat2 — b1577eb7-ca0a-4924-9a9b-caa2c5ef0aa6

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/1065b8d4-17cf-4519-824e-727e44ea884b) · match `b1577eb7-ca0a-4924-9a9b-caa2c5ef0aa6` · 2026-10-29T12:10:04.358Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 655 rounds · loss. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**SSSA wins 1024–147.**

SSSA defeats higher-ranked Sponge 1024–147 with only 1915 versus 400 earned gold across the teams, a low-economy setting where killing and protecting gem carriers have high value. Marcella gets the first champion kill at R39, and the winner's banked score is 252–63 by R250 and 723–105 by R500. Sponge loses 63 on Heart at R142 and 77 on Stellar at R345, and suffers eleven deaths versus four. SSSA's Marcella develops Prospector at R150 and Capture/Jungle at R191 and eventually banks 358. Low-cost effective capture and delivery matter here; assuming a long compound-economy horizon would misprice the contest. The rare Stellar pick loses this game but wins reverse_spiral, so the evidence supports context dependence rather than a categorical ban. A future draft test must hold the farming and carrier-protection controller fixed.

## 23. 00rows — cf10b651-6721-430f-b463-d35858b12843

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/376b6b92-636a-45e3-ab69-91c0c6fbac87) · match `cf10b651-6721-430f-b463-d35858b12843` · 2026-10-29T12:10:04.343Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1384 rounds · loss. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Supaswag wins 1006–983.**

Supaswag wins a23-point finish despite less gold 11250–11602, fewer collected gems 1006–1009 and one death against zero. hello leads 94–63 score at R250 and 259–163 at R500; Supaswag takes a lasting lead only at R708. Marcella supplies 455 submitted gems and the finishing 12 at R1384, while hello's final paired Cassi/Issac banks of 12/26 at R1376 leave it on 983. There is no large carrier death to explain away the result: the only champion kill at R142 takes a zero-gem Vesper. This is the clearest pure delivery-throughput counterexample in the set. The appropriate optimisation is remaining completion time and useful bag size, not maximising income or eliminating all deaths. Which exact earlier route or channel start would win for hello is an unresolved, narrowly testable counterfactual.

## 24. 01elk_arena — 24b6f8f8-87d8-4253-bddd-94b3b9bda1dd

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/6c8a9b9b-285e-4a73-a0b3-078a6a5f76e5) · match `24b6f8f8-87d8-4253-bddd-94b3b9bda1dd` · 2026-10-29T12:20:04.699Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1777 rounds · loss. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**procless wins 1046–144.**

procless, currently lower-ranked, beats Sponge 1046–144 through sustained production and broad conversion. At R100 it already leads gold 792–520 and score 72–0; by R250 its gold lead is 2528–1308. All five winning bodies complete Prospector as their first emblem, including Vesper at R61, and the group-rich map contains qualifying cluster income as well as monsters. Vesper earns 4402 and banks 246, but the score is distributed; repeated late 82-gem submissions by Ryth and Vendetta close the match. Sponge's Marcella dies at R190 and its side eventually loses 24 bodies versus 18; Vendetta later drops 72 at R1051. The first 72-point lead alone is not a runaway score advantage—both sides are tied 72 at R500—so production takes time to become delivery. This supports testing cluster-aware reward allocation rather than indiscriminate funneling.

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/79a027a1-da45-4fb9-a22f-a75bd485d1a3) · match `1def0ee1-3ec7-4bd8-b7d8-184a3c033640` · 2026-10-29T12:00:04.802Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 906 rounds · elite-fallback. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**NEETCoders wins 1139–683.**

NEETCoders starts scoring at R40 and keeps the lead. Its Vesper opens Prospector at R66, Treasure at R132 and Jungle at R196, then becomes the main banker with 531 submitted. At R250 score is 120–0 and gold 1175–802, and at R500 score 414–114. Deaths end equal at three; the first champion kill is not until R462. The decisive repeatable pattern is capture selection, credit allocation and timely trips rather than superior kill count. The winning final 235-gem Vesper bank at R906 produces 1139 total score. Its opponent also uses Prospector openings, so merely copying that family cannot explain the difference. Evaluate the actual capture shares and route completion times that finance Vesper, then test a different banking scheduler while preserving the rest of the controller.

## 26. 01yellow_brick_road — 89903723-f08f-49a8-af2a-bf2602c3a3f2

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/785df47b-6262-4298-850c-d470c25fee89) · match `89903723-f08f-49a8-af2a-bf2602c3a3f2` · 2026-10-29T12:50:04.701Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 1046 rounds · elite-fallback. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Intercontinental Fish wins 1009–727.**

Intercontinental Fish wins 1009–727 despite ten deaths against Mite's three and losing the first champion kill at R39. Fish leads early gold, but the score race still changes: the permanent winning lead begins at R708. Its Vesper loses 94 at R610 yet ends with 6087 earned gold and 341 submitted gems; secondary bodies remain productive enough to finish, including Issac's45 at R1046. Mite records 77 fuel rollovers while Fish records none, but rollovers alone cannot be declared causal because the draft, route and policy differ. This is evidence that protecting one headline statistic—low deaths, first blood, or a single carry's bag—is insufficient. The resilient production portfolio and later delivery sequence are more explanatory. Test decision freshness under fuel suspension separately from a draft or aggression change.

## 27. 02treasure_box — 371424db-f12f-44cc-8a6f-4e440267a4ed

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/04fe754e-c02e-402d-9cb0-8e62b274dcc8) · match `371424db-f12f-44cc-8a6f-4e440267a4ed` · 2026-10-29T12:10:04.315Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 866 rounds · elite-fallback. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**prism best girl wins 1003–909.**

The rank-one developer team wins after allowing newer team a substantial score lead: at R500 it trails 264–528 but leads gold 5161–2235 and equipment 4920–1950. It takes the permanent score lead only at R816 and wins 1003–909 at R866. Its Vesper earns 6854 and banks 426, while the side suffers zero deaths against fourteen. The first kill at R26 is Vendetta eliminating the opposing Vesper; later repeated losses constrain the challenger's ability to finish its early lead. Newer team also has 43 fuel rollovers, versus none for the winner, an associated execution difference that needs a separate causal test. This case explains why delayed banking can be viable with protected economic dominance, and why it is dangerous to infer a universal small-bank rule from successful early leaders elsewhere.

## 28. 00packs — 1bf7d5f5-21a3-4f08-9241-ab9cddda593b

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/e5014c75-f2e8-40d0-9b92-787c83f63e07) · match `1bf7d5f5-21a3-4f08-9241-ab9cddda593b` · 2026-10-29T12:40:05.684Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 997 rounds · elite-fallback. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Mite wins 1004–188.**

Mite and Claudus use the same Vesper–Heart–Vendetta draft, but Mite wins 1004–188. R100 incomes are almost equal (116–111), with Mite having no equipment against 90; by R250 Mite leads 656–457 gold and 100–64 score. Mite opens Jungle on its developing bodies, while Claudus opens Treasure, and Mite's final deaths are two against eight. Claudus wins the first champion kill at R272, but its Heart loses 28 immediately at R273; the later production and delivery gap persists. This is direct evidence that a mirrored popular draft and first blood do not identify the stronger controller. The best-supported transferable target is camp survival and repeated completion, with role-specific items. An isolated Jungle-versus-Treasure experiment is justified on this geometry, but its effect cannot be separated from pathing and combat in the observed game.

## 29. 02uncharted_lands — 86025019-01bb-49d8-9dd8-f328dc853beb

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/446ff67a-3981-4c87-a8bd-7c3f7649a854) · match `86025019-01bb-49d8-9dd8-f328dc853beb` · 2026-10-29T12:20:04.659Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 704 rounds · elite-fallback. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**print dont int wins 1010–902.**

print dont int wins 1010–902 after trailing the score at R500 (561–598). The teams' R250 income is almost tied 1009–1011, and the winner has less equipment 720–840. Its Marcella opens Treasure at R122 and Durability at R171, then eventually earns 2634 and banks 466. A zero-gem Vendetta kill at R493 does not itself explain the score swing; the lasting lead starts at R568 and the final 62 arrives via Marcella at R704. Newer team instead shows early Prospector Vesper and Hunter Vendetta, illustrating two economically plausible plans with different conversion outcomes. This is a close-race case for completion-time estimates and protected delivery, rather than a broad claim that Treasure defeats Prospector. Counterfactuals should focus on the final channel/route schedule before changing composition.

## 30. 02crossing — 6ec7890f-98ad-411f-afb3-1c111a31b850

[Official group and replay](https://2026a.battlecode.co.uk/game/match-groups/6520e8ed-d851-47ed-b619-bd129561fad4) · match `6ec7890f-98ad-411f-afb3-1c111a31b850` · 2026-10-29T12:30:04.613Z · engine 2.0.0/build 85aa7167 · 588 rounds · elite-fallback. Submission versions are hidden, so both are unknown. Snapshot ranks are selection-time ranks, not pregame ratings.

**Trzy Kwaterki wins 1025–124.**

Trzy Kwaterki establishes a large early conversion lead, 125–0 score and 523–170 gold by R100, with a first bank at R63. Its Marcella opens Treasure at R82 and Prospector at R168 and eventually contributes 605 of 1025 submitted gems and 3086 earned gold. Supaswag suffers thirteen deaths versus four and loses 58 on Marcella at R368 and 65 on Vesper at R534. Those concrete bag losses accompany repeated production downtime, rather than merely a final item deficit. The leader never gives up the score lead after R63 and closes through multiple bodies, including Marcella's final 58 at R588. The result supports an opening that connects captures to safe delivery quickly when geometry permits it. It does not justify always rushing an altar on distant or gated maps; contest and boss_battle provide clear counterconditions.
